# M-POC5A.4d3 — 59 pozycji / 118 rzeczywistych porównań

Rzeczywiste porównanie59 przydzielonych pozycji/118 wystąpień obu hashów. Obejrzano dostarczonePDF34–35, diagram i istniejący wycinek Step4 zPDF27; przeczytano każdy dokładny source child, pełne mapped pola obu odpowiedzi i ich różnice. Osiem source_transcription prowadzi przed kopertą parent; child przecinający kolumny łączono w zapisanej kolejności. SEM-021 dotyczy jednego wyjaśnienia historycznego w dwóch wynikach, parent i child nie tworzą podwójnych błędów.

118 ocen zakresów inwentarza, nie118 reguł/TP lub nowych punktów rubryki. Oznaczenie nie akceptuje całego parent. Wybrana klauzula i warunki w powiązanych polach oceniane razem. Streszczenia/structured diagram nie są pełną transkrypcją.114 propozycji ograniczonego wsparcia oraz2mieszaneparent+2pominiętechild to118 zakresów; jedna pominięta klauzula występuje w obu odpowiedziach. Starsze pending, wyłączone pola i pełne wyniki pozostają otwarte.

## C-9.32-01 — clause_group

ObaR932 zachowują attackingLC i PH/HI/MI/LG/BI oraz half/floor Hits zadawanych. ## R-9.41 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.41 odpowiadająPhalanxDefense naPDF34. Numer poprawny, nie pełnyCRT.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.41 In any Shock Resolution in which a unit moves adjacent to
> and Shock attacks a Phalanx from the defender’s Front hex, the
> Phalanx earns a 2L shift on the Shock Combat CRT for each of
> its Flank hexes either occupied by another friendly Phalanx unit
> or in the ZOC of a friendly Phalanx unit. If the attacker is Heavy
> Infantry (any Class), the shift is 1L instead. This applies only to
> moving attacks, not to attacks in which the attacking unit started
> adjacent to the Phalanx unit and stayed in that hex to Shock. It
> also does not apply to Flank/Rear attacks, or if any attacker is
> also a Phalanx unit.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-left.txt`, znaki[914, 1518], hash`e3d2ed171bf21d3272f9a079fdd55287e5a95831143abcf35cbc3f2ce7987422`.

## R-9.51 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.51 wskazują formationDD. Tylko oznaczenie, nie konkretną scenario eligibility.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.51 Phalanxes may use Double-Depth (DD) formation, which al-
> lows them to stack two to a hex. Forming Double-Depth requires
> an Individual Order or Line Command, directly from the OC. To
> form Double-Depth, the stacking, “back” Phalanx must start its
> movement in the hex directly behind the “front” Phalanx. The
> “back” Phalanx then moves into the front Phalanx’s hex, paying
> normal terrain costs, and expends 1 MP in addition to the terrain
> cost to stack. The “back” Phalanx is then placed underneath the
> “front” Phalanx. This completes the movement for both units
> for that Orders Phase.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[265, 851], hash`a1ccb3fa8c778709113df8d914b298a1be8d3f020e9397823e42a9d1a0a9a693`.

## R-9.52 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.52 odpowiadająMovementRestrictionsDD, nie rzymskim zwykłym stosom.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.52 Movement Restrictions: Double Depth formation has the
> following effects:

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[852, 929], hash`f88be05b7b5c0d57d77e28ed85951fbf0c95fbc144ba29cb5a44c6b16bbc858f`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • The units may receive orders only from the OC.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[930, 978], hash`b1d208553da18ad24506033edc6461b76bdea08fc7ead1badd99517617ec1446`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • They may move as one unit, but it takes 2 Individual Orders
> (when using Individual Orders) to move both units together.
> They may use Pre-Arranged Withdrawal (6.8).

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[979, 1144], hash`24cae6d212b4c5616264a92bfeb15874264b7c03e03d4ddf954a75d0d53c5fea`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • The units may not Wheel (6.44), Reverse (6.45), or use Col-
> umn (6.7). Unstacking requires an Individual Order or Line
> Command as above, at which time the top unit moves off at
> normal costs. The bottom unit must remain in place.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1145, 1375], hash`64331eaf9d9b7fbb9ac843fc1c621fb7eac32630bcc0fc0474254763781e4302`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • The units always use their 4 MA; they never use their 5 MA
> for anything. For any movement that would incur a Cohesion
> Hit, both units incur the Hit.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1376, 1526], hash`a9290462d2438ea4775a51f1f79f639c626a75e7cf11d871c0c66a4134e2579b`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • No unit, of any type, may ever move through or into a Double
> Depth stack. Treat them as impassable terrain.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1527, 1636], hash`2addea9dfa9b1adda95fe48cdd473b2df7b479ce022d3318933b0a903b730b31`.

## R-9.53 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.53 odpowiadająCombatEffectsDD. Oznaczenie nie przyjmuje niejednoznacznej końcowej liczbyeliminowanychunits.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.53 Combat Effects: Double Depth formation has the follow-
> ing effects on combat:

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1637, 1719], hash`40e085aab9ff4eed48f9672582bd4516c26b7204420428ed0db24be5622c605e`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • The Sizes of both units are combined.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1720, 1759], hash`526b7715b622ad07b3accf5060ec340964470e6c7558b7e83121dcc6ba8a4528`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • Only the top unit is used for TQ checks (unless attacked from
> the rear, in which case it’s the bottom unit). However, if that
> unit incurs any Cohesion Hits, then the other unit checks.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1760, 1946], hash`c34990bffbae2264a59682d2f0c93c85c7e50daa97679cd3d633018bd3477494`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • Units defending against a Shock attack by units in Double
> Depth incur a +1 DRM to their Pre-Shock TQ check die rolls.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[1947, 2066], hash`ba243ef546cbc42cc61f6a7ed5f75afbe8c8091bfe7357fe53de0560e30f6408`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • Defending units at Double Depth earn a –1 DRM to their Pre-
> Shock TQ check die rolls. Phalanx Defense (9.41) also applies.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[2067, 2191], hash`5d9c208f6b24ff09172c3b3d6d754eec80d98345c8d6abd0db95475c8f88e445`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • If a Double Depth stack is Shock attacked from a Flank or
> Rear hex, the defender Cohesion Hits are tripled (3x) instead
> of doubled.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[2192, 2325], hash`d6c73d06a30da7b1e26b175811c005fe6a41d39637681b390a41380807235a20`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • Cohesion Hits from Shock are distributed equally between the
> two units, with any odd Hits going to the top unit. Cohesion Hits
> from Missile Fire Hits apply only to the top unit unless fire is
> through a Rear hex, in which case Hits apply to the bottom unit.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[2326, 2584], hash`0ff172aa04aa9f825410b73a5d5aa7ded6e2c94aec876205b2f3c32a2ac311af`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • If either unit in the stack routs, they both rout. The units must
> retreat to separate hexes. If this is not possible, the unit is
> eliminated instead.

PDF34, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-34-right.txt`, znaki[2585, 2736], hash`8283ca03c6b34e2e0b07db1b6629f0c7b6d99a031e059441286dd2a41bb15aa8`.

## C-9.53-02 — clause_group

ObaactionR953 combineSizes obu jednostek. Nie combineTQ lub wszystkie ratings.

Wynik: bounded_clause_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

## C-9.53-07 — clause_group

ObaR953 dzieląShockHits equally/oddtop, MissileHitstop exceptRearbottom. Nie zamieniono allocationreguł obu rodzajówFire. Wartości i wykonawcy zachowane.

Wynik: bounded_clause_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> • Cohesion Hits from Shock are distributed equally between the
> two units, with any odd Hits going to the top unit. Cohesion Hits
> from Missile Fire Hits apply only to the top unit unless fire is
> through a Rear hex, in which case Hits apply to the bottom unit.

## R-9.61 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.61 odpowiadająRomanstacksummary. Numer nie jest nowym przyjęciem stackingcharts/wyjątków.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.61 See the Stacking Charts for a summary of the legionary
> stacking advantages. For Romans, the rule of thumb is that two
> Roman infantry units of the same border color can stack without
> penalty. There are exceptions, such as Velites. A Roman stack
> may move together when necessary or desired. It takes two In-
> dividual Orders to move two stacked units (only one, if stacked
> with the OC, and he is giving the orders), but only one LC, if they
> are both eligible. Roman infantry units are also allowed spaces
> between them when determining lines; see 4.32.

## R-9.62 — rule_label

> 9.62 Stacked Roman units combine their Size when attacking or
> defending, but all other ratings, especially TQ, are those of the
> top unit. Stacked Romans must Shock attack the same hex; see

> check but uses the TQ of the top unit. Automatic Cohesion Hits
> (6.68) are applied to each unit in the stack. Cohesion Hits from
> Shock resolution 8.46 Step 4 are allocated per 10.13.

## R-9.66 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.66 poprawnie lokalizująMLE przez kolumnyPDF35. Każdychild i diagram osobno.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.66 Manipular Line Extension: Stacked Roman units with
> unoccupied flank hexes can extend their line as part of a Line
> Command or in reaction to enemy movement, instead of using
> Orderly Withdrawal. To undertake Manipular Line Extension
> (MLE), all the top units in the stacks in the same Line (see 4.32)

PDF35, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-35-left.txt`, znaki[2061, 2363], hash`78f078da6fa2fe33ab8e624792fedfa3a278aab91723954cab2e31c9131f6459`.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> may move sideways into the vacant flank hexes (see the diagram
> above). If a Flank hex is occupied, MLE cannot be used to place
> a unit in that hex. The hex entered by MLE must be Clear and
> not in an enemy ZOC. Facing remains the same. This rule only
> applies to unstacking the units; it does not apply to stacking
> them together.

PDF35, `private/poc/spqr-chapter9/source/review-evidence/pdf-35-right.txt`, znaki[0, 326], hash`cfd9337e2ded13cc74961ce9004fcd4ab693a1f1f7b83429434743eeb5c951a4`.

> 9.66 Manipular Line Extension: Stacked Roman units with
> unoccupied flank hexes can extend their line as part of a Line
> Command or in reaction to enemy movement, instead of using
> Orderly Withdrawal. To undertake Manipular Line Extension
> (MLE), all the top units in the stacks in the same Line (see 4.32)

> applies to unstacking the units; it does not apply to stacking
> them together.

## R-9.67 — rule_label

Oba original_label=9.67 odpowiadająreactiveMLE, nieLCpostmovement9.68.

Wynik: bounded_label_supported; oba warianty ocenione, owner waiting_review, cały rekord nieprzyjęty.

Dokładny fragment źródła:

> 9.67 Manipular Line Extension may be undertaken as a reaction
> to enemy movement the instant an enemy combat unit comes
> within 2 hexes of a line of stacked Roman units. The Roman
> player may make his choice at any time any enemy unit so places
> itself (that is, he does not have to exercise his MLE rights the first
> time they become available). If enemy movement triggers both
> Orderly Withdrawal as well as MLE, both may be undertaken
> together but not by the same units.
